{"forms":[{"form":"Kelvin functions","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["Any of class of special functions, usually denoted as two pairs of functions berₙ(x), beiₙ(x), kerₙ(x) and keiₙ(x) with variable x and given order number n. {"forms":[{"form":"the commutant lifting theorem","tags":["canonical"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"name","senses":[{"glosses":["A theorem in operator theory, stating that, if T is a contraction on a Hilbert space H, and U is its minimal unitary dilation acting on some Hilbert space K, and R is an operator on H commuting with T, then there is an operator S on K commuting with U such that RTⁿ=P_HSUⁿ|_H;∀n≥0, and ‖S‖=‖R‖. In other words, an operator from the commutant of T can be \"lifted\" to an operator in the commutant of the unitary dilation of T."],"topics":["mathematics","sciences"]}],"upstream_line":5415246,"upstream_offset":12606583225,"upstream_record_sha256":"3af066fea23dbc609fa18a3ceee29a16b0113f699b82c9b6c5c24ff401bcf81c","word":"commutant lifting theorem"}
